# Frontier analysis, families 071–086

Two complete technical readings produced two reusable deductions. No
publication, external validation, or global resolution of the assigned
conjectures is claimed. All sources are pinned to OpenAI/math commit
`adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a` (commit timestamp
2026-10-06T21:58:50Z in the shared commit record). No nested workers,
untrusted build-script execution, paid services, contacts, or commits
were used.
The root dispatch record identifies this new worker's configuration as
`gpt-6.1-sol`, reasoning effort `max`, with no model fallback.

The strongest result is the extracted Schatten-3 triangle certificate.
The entire dyadic triangular manuscript (October 5, 2026) was read and
its finite matrix, conditional-child, total-energy, telescoping and
density steps reconstructed. The same proof extends its constant 40
to every base q≥2, complex L³ inputs, and arbitrary real child-constant
mean-zero multipliers bounded by one. The q-independent step is a
two-way Jensen rounding of the q-way partition. The complex phase
mechanism also appears in the annular companion's matrix section,
which was subsequently read in full; that part is not a new priority
claim. The q-uniform extension is a deduction of the release mechanism,
with broader novelty unresolved.

Pairwise independence of child coordinates is essential. Uniform
marginals alone permit an exact 2×2 example with local trace −1/8 and
energy gap 3/4−√2<0. This records a concrete obstruction to transferring
the dyadic proof to arbitrary correlated or geometric refinements.

The second result is a generalized capacity decomposition. The entire
diagonal restriction manuscript (September 24, 2026) was read. Its
Lemma 3.2 extends to any positive-density exponent r>1: for n weighted
atoms and a legal geometric test capacity, there are at most
`min(n,2+floor((1+1/(r−1))log₂ n))` disjoint coordinate pieces with

```
Σ_j m(u^j)K(u^j)^{r−1}
 ≤ [4^{r−1}C_geo^{r−1}+1] ∫H^r.
```

The bound has no dependence on coefficient dynamic range. If weights
are squared complex coefficients and the oscillatory map is linear,
the output norm power is 2r and the resulting grouping loss is
J^{2r−1}; r=3/2 reproduces the source's log² loss. The capacity oracle's
acquisition cost remains separate from the logarithmic piece count.

The full sharp restriction claim remains conditional in this report on
the sphere packet propagation Theorem 6.4 and the 3D Kakeya maximal
Theorem 1.1. I read the exact packet interface and targeted portions of
its extremal/time-profile argument. At the pinned version the packet
interface explicitly allows transition-annulus frequency labels, so the
diagonal paper's insertion of arbitrary elliptic graph germs has the
advertised legal domain; an objection based solely on sphere-only
labels would be incorrect. The complete underlying geometric
propagation and refined quartic packet estimates were not independently
checked. The strict restriction conclusion also supplies no p=3
endpoint and no polynomial bound on its full constant as p↓3, because
packet/Kakeya loss-constant growth is unspecified.

The source ledger verified selected manuscript authors and dates from
main/README files, and older primary titles, authors, version histories,
and precise relevant statements. KTZ 2015 equations (1.5)–(1.6) and
Theorem 1.7 verify the earlier cyclic trace model and restricted input
interface; Kovač 2011 Section 6.2 verifies the older non-bipartite
obstruction. Wang–Wu arXiv:2411.08871v3, dated December 19, 2024,
Theorem 0.2 provides the closest cited p>22/7 comparator. This is not
a complete search of every later primary restriction result.

# Family116 characteristic-zero independent check

The characteristic-zero source proof survives an independent reconstruction of monodromy, field transfer, formula/ABP conversion, Wronskian/minor multiplicity, and rational bit bounds. This is internal proof review, with no fresh Lean build or external validation. The Lean scope document asserts universal detection but excludes the quantitative construction/dimension statements from its selected theorem.

The strongest new deduction uses **full evaluated matrices**. The source's explicit Volterra tuple at dimension `2nm` hits every nonzero polynomial with an acyclic `m`-state coefficient realization, improving its quadratic first-column dimension to linear full-response dimension. Thus dimension `4ns` suffices for the source's size-`s` formula trees. The proof reconstructs a nonzero bivariate Volterra kernel, bounds a diagonal derivative by rational row-span closure, and recovers its first homogeneous part with invertible Cauchy moments. It uses the source's finite-word faithfulness, but does not use the scalar Moura bound. Root, experiments, analysis and crossfield independently reconstructed the core argument. These internal audits are not external validation; historical novelty remains UNKNOWN.

For arbitrary proper cyclic `m`-state recognizable series, the fixed **m-point scalar grid** `ell T_i`, `ell=1,...,m`, each at dimension `2nm`, hits every nonzero series. The derivative numerator is divisible by the length marker lambda, and after division has lambda-degree at most `m-1`; a nonzero coefficient therefore survives some grid point. Specialization does not use rational row-dependence coefficients. Alternatively, a single nilpotent marker tuple has dimension `2nm(m+1)`, or the grid direct sum has dimension `2nm^2`. Every query is jointly nilpotent and defined under proper-series semantics.

The exact output-only residual learner must retain all matrix entries. Full-width prefix chains and the scalar grid recover a minimal `r<=m` weighted automaton using at most `m(1+nr)` exact queries of dimension at most `2nm(r+1)`, feature length `4n^2m^3`, plus polynomial exact field work. Residual combinations share hidden transitions, so `m` suffices; comparison with an unrelated m-state hypothesis instead needs `2m`. A conservative `2m` learner has at most `2m(1+nr)` queries, dimension at most `4nm(r+1)`, feature length `32n^2m^3`. Individual scalar-seed queries offer a different matrix-size/query-count tradeoff, fully ledgered but not implemented here. All full responses, description sizes, response heights and oracle work are charged. There is no coefficient-bit bound from `n,m` alone.

The independent proof, alias and complete ledger are in `notes/frontier_check_hitting/cycle19/FULL_MATRIX_VOLterra_AND_CYCLIC_ALIAS.md`. New code `work/frontier_check_hitting/cycle19/reconstruct_scalar_grid.py` passes three exact fixtures: native two-state cycles (10 calls, maximum dimension 24), the same target with conservative comparison bound `2m` (20 calls, dimension 48), and a reversed full-operator alias (rank four, 45 calls, dimension 80). A separate reachable-space closure certifies all-word equality, and finite Hankel minors certify fixture minimality. Full data and bit payload costs are saved in `SCALAR_GRID_RECONSTRUCTION_CHECK.json`. These runs test the implementation; they do not replace the proof.

The original first-column length-marker consequence and learner remain separately saved in `CHARZERO_AUDIT_AND_MATRIX_ORACLE_ACQUISITION.md`, `work/frontier_check_hitting/cycle19/reconstruct_abp.py`, and `RECONSTRUCTION_CHECK.json`. Nine original fixtures pass all-word closure certificates; crossfield independently passed eight fresh adversarial fixtures. Their evidence is not relabeled as a test of the new full-response algorithm.

Two general adversarial deductions sharpen the interface. A suitable linear combination of `n` kernels has first jet at order `n`, attaining the `m=2` bound. All degree-`2k` binary homogeneous polynomials fit `m=3*2^k-2` acyclic states, so any faithful fixed first-column feature needs dimension at least `4^k=(m+2)^2/9`. The source's quadratic state scale is therefore near optimal for that particular interface.

W-frontier-crossfield | frontier crossfield

# Cross-field frontier reset: exact recognizable-series acquisition

**Strongest supported consequence:** family116's finite-word faithfulness plus a newly reconstructed Volterra-kernel argument gives a much smaller exact cyclic detector. For a characteristic-zero proper m-state recognizable series over n letters, the explicit tuple T of dimension 2nm detects every nonzero series at some scalar multiple ell T, ell∈{1,...,m}, using the full matrix response. A nilpotent length marker of dimension 2nm(m+1) also works. This repairs an explicit proper cyclic series that vanishes as an entire unmarked Volterra operator, not merely one seed. Root's scalar-grid proof and the peer's kernel argument were independently reconstructed here; historical priority is unestablished.

The detector compiles an output-only exact residual learner. If the true residual rank is r≤m, batching 2n suffix seeds per query gives at most m+nm²r queries, each of matrix dimension≤2n(m+r)≤4nm, and returns a minimal r-state weighted realization. Every returned matrix entry is charged: the worst nominal response payload is O(n³m⁵), with straightforward coordinate processing O(n³m⁶) field operations and oracle execution charged separately. Larger seed batches trade fewer calls for larger matrices. Exact rational response processing is polynomial in encoded response length; m alone does not bound hidden heights. The precise proof and implementation are in the new addendum. The preceding first-column detector/learner and its eight-fixture independent audit are preserved as a separate packet.

**Significance is scoped.** This is a reusable exact algebraic primitive for ordered sequential computations whose inputs can be replaced by rational matrices. It does not resolve the programme’s teacher-free statistical approximation or abelianized commutative-PIT targets. Historical novelty is not cleared, and neither the release nor our agreement is external validation.

**Prior-comparison correction:** release116 already implies polynomial proper-cyclic detection through standard truncation/unrolling: an m-state series is nonzero iff its length<m part is nonzero, which has an m²-state acyclic realization. Apply the released tuple T there, then query the actual cyclic oracle at S_m tensor T, of dimension O(nm⁵). Independent marker powers separate the homogeneous responses. The same first-column residual construction supplies a routine polynomial exact-acquisition baseline. Our substantive contribution is smaller dimensions and charged query construction, not the first polynomial cyclic capability. Full derivation: `notes/frontier_crossfield/cycle19/RELEASE116_TRUNCATION_BASELINE_CORRECTION.md`.



## Main technical packet

Read the full characteristic-zero paper: Theorem1.1; finite-word independence Lemma2.1; formula/path representation Lemma3.1 and Proposition3.2; Moura multiplicity Theorem4.2; acyclic hitting Proposition5.1. Exact labels are preserved in the note. I read the general rational-formula companion's introduction/theorem and master, not its full proof; I did not fully read the positive-characteristic companion or run Lean. The subsequently read `lean/docs/116.md` lists a characteristic-zero formula-hitting comparator, and says its polynomial construction and O(ns²) dimension bounds are not separately asserted. The rational-list comparator includes polynomial bit bounds. Coverage documentation is not an independently replayed formal proof.

## Independent deductions that constrain further transfer

# Dynamics frontier cycle19 report

Scoped investigation finished for families143–154. All twelve overview/CONTENTS entries were read; complete technical TeX readings were performed for148 and149. Family154 introduction and dynamical-input section were read, with remaining sources downloaded but not adopted. No nested workers, publication, contact, commits, or shared-file edits were used. Pinned release: `adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a`.

The strongest outcome is an explicit independently derived affine certificate for a planar polynomial family where the standard centered entropy function fails as an exterior Lyapunov function. With six rates varying arbitrarily and measurably by ±10% around `(a,b,c,d,u,v)=(2,7,7,2,1,1)`,

    x'=g, y'=−g+u−vy, g=−ax³+bx²y−cxy²+dy³,

has the global envelope

    F=min(0,x−1/16,y−x/4,x+y−1/2,10−x−y,4x+2−y,8−x).

Every active nonconstant branch increases at least `2379/40960` outside its plateau. The plateau is an invariant absorbing pentagon, with exact bounds `x∈[1/16,8]`, `y∈[1/10,42/5]`, and entry time at most `−F(x0,y0)/(2379/40960)`. This statement is proved by universal polynomial inequalities and a Carathéodory chain rule, independently of the new release. Exact rational code checks all64 rate corners, all five vertices, and the constants. The reaction realization supplied here has three reversible linkage classes; the baseline field matches [Boros–Hofbauer Section7.2](https://arxiv.org/html/1903.03071v2#S7.SS2), whose qualitative permanence was already known. No new general permanence theorem or prior-art priority is asserted.

A decisive mechanism detail is that `8−x` is geometrically redundant for the plateau but required for this global active-region proof. Removing it preserves the pentagon but makes the five-branch envelope decrease at `(100,1/10)`. Adding redundant affine branches can therefore repair a certificate's dynamics without changing its target set. This is a concrete route toward symbolic synthesis on other systems with failed smooth entropy barriers.

Full source149 reconstruction also yields a conditional general extension: assuming its arbitrary-tolerance affine approximation lemma, one common convex absorbing set works uniformly over all measurable rates in `[k_-,k_+]`, and the complex vectors may be real rather than nonnegative integers, provided kinetic exponents equal source complex coordinates. The explicit inward derivative bound is

    δ_h=2H(k_-−|R|k_+h^H)h^M,
    M=max_y ||y||1.

This general statement remains conditional on independently auditing affine Lemma2.1; its initial technical reconstruction found no gap. Source149's [Lean scope documentation](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/lean/docs/149.md) covers only the September25 initial-condition-dependent predecessor, not October5 classwise permanence. This distinction must remain in root's assessment.

Full source148 reading isolated a second reusable mechanism: finite-law hidden entropy plus small sumset size forces fair-pair capacity across finite fine-scale bands; adapted disjoint windows turn that capacity into a contradiction without an upper growth bound for the bands' endpoints. This can reopen scale arguments blocked on effective minimum separation, when they establish the exact conditional-gain interface. It supplies no uniform bit-time or observation-depth consequence.

Family154 has additional new theorem dependencies: source145 all-orders mixing, and the distinct-slopes companion's smooth triple oscillation theorem on arbitrary invertible systems. The latter already implies triple pointwise convergence without mixing. These proofs are not reproduced in154 and were not audited here. Source148's Lean comparator/main wrapper was inspected and matches the actual Hausdorff-dimension scope; no transitive Lean build was performed.

## Strongest mathematical outcome

Exact full-output rank experiments suggested a stronger use of the release's explicit rational matrices. The resulting independently reconstructed proof gives **dimension d=2nm** for complete-matrix detection of every nonzero characteristic-zero polynomial with an acyclic m-state coefficient realization f_w=uB_wv, with strictly upper triangular B_i. The generator reads only n,m. For the source's division-free ordered formula trees, counting every leaf and gate and using m<=2s, **d=4ns** suffices. The source's first-column theorem uses quadratic dimension in m or s.

The proof uses the ordered bivariate Volterra kernel K(z,t)=uG(z)G(t)^(-1)A(t)v, a short rational covariant row span, a nonzero diagonal derivative of total degree at most nm-1, and invertible Cauchy moments of several input seeds. It uses source finite-word hyperlogarithm faithfulness and formula-to-acyclic representation; it does not use the source's scalar Moura multiplicity bound or its Lean label as a proof substitute.

**Internal mathematical PASS:** root, frontier_analysis and frontier_check_hitting saved independent reconstructions of the entire proof, including the source faithfulness prerequisite in the latter audits. This is neither external nor formal validation; historical novelty is UNKNOWN. The hostile audit additionally gives d=2n(m-1) for m>=2 by restricting the kernel system to an invariant subspace, with constants handled separately. The saved experiments use conservative d=2nm throughout.

The gain concerns matrix dimension and dense query description. At fixed n, the old first-column response and the new full response both retain order m^2 field elements. An explicit m-state class containing all binary homogeneous degree-2t polynomials, with m=3*2^t-2, proves a quadratic fixed-seed dimension lower bound and a linear full-matrix dimension lower bound. Thus the new full-matrix order is optimal in m at fixed n, and the old first-column order is also optimal for its own interface. No automatic total information, runtime or learner-query improvement follows.

Complete derivation: [LINEAR_DIMENSION_CANDIDATE.md](../../../notes/frontier_experiments/cycle19/LINEAR_DIMENSION_CANDIDATE.md). ## Exact cyclic obstruction and repair

The nonzero proper free series

    R=x1*x2-x2*(1+x1)^(-1)*x1*(1+x2)

has leading commutator but evaluates to the zero full operator at J_1,J_2, and hence at every finite unmarked T tuple. The formal proof uses differentiation and zero initial values; dimensions 2 through 25 also replay exactly. This refutes the naive cyclic extension even when all matrix entries are read.

Root's length-grading deduction repairs this obstruction: every nonzero proper recognizable m-state series is hit by one of **m predetermined tuples ell*T_i, ell=1,...,m, each of dimension 2nm**. Each evaluation exists by joint nilpotence. The proof bounds a selected derivative numerator's lambda-degree after removing an explicit lambda factor, so it cannot vanish at all m scales; it does not bound the degree of the entire output. One direct-sum tuple has dimension 2nm^2. The two written peer audits reconstruct this theorem. This is a proper-series result and does not assume arbitrary rational-expression domains.

The preregistered exact discriminator executed all 14 scales across three cyclic realizations. A five-state alias at d=20 has the entire ell=1 response zero and diagnostic entry ell^2(ell-1)/24, giving the first hit 1/6 at ell=2. A dense three-state nonzero is detected already at ell=1. A six-state doubled dense cancellation zero remains zero at all six scales. Direct expression evaluation, a separate Neumann evaluator and supplied-realization coefficient truth independently check the main block solve.

# Frontier methods checkpoint19

**Incremental reusable mechanisms were reconstructed and tested; no foundational
breakthrough or general discovery improvement was established.** I read three
released reasoning-summary families, with full summaries007/102 and substantial
excerpts159. The complete core proof of the Unique Games manuscript was read;
external shortcode and auxiliary consequence gates remain unverified.

The main mathematical output is a charged procedure combining positive
Fourier-moment repair with unconditional signed witness transfer. If c_S are
supplied exact character expectations, q=sum|c_S| and H=sum c_S chi_S, then
Q=(P+(q-H)U)/(1+q) is a probability law with zero selected moments and
TV(P,Q)<=q/(1+q). A nested witness surplus P[J intersect M]-aP[J] loses at most
TV(P,Q) under transfer. These sharpen the source's elementary bounds, but the
repair is exactly Alon-Goldreich-Mansour's2002/2003 prior construction after
flattening sequential parity mixtures. It is not a new method or headline proof.

Eight frozen exact cases passed normalization, positivity, moment, and TV checks.
The equally informed exact minimum-TV control was strictly better five times and
tied three; clipping/normalization violated target moments twice. All eight LP
optima have independently verified exact rational primal/dual certificates.
The first SymPy control proposal was invalid and rejected; its failure is retained.
The rare-event fixture has TV1/1024 but conditional TV1. Exact moment acquisition
from finite iid data cannot be silently assumed; a Bernoulli two-law calculation
also charges approximate acquisition at Omega(delta^-2). The support and
conditioning costs prevent automatic transfer to the imported discrepancy/HMM
or sparse progression problems.

A more distinctive mechanism is102's harmonic rank potential. The source's
one-rank conditional hazard proportional to r*theta is cancelled by a1/r
potential increment. The generic conditional theorem is reconstructed in the
note. An exact pure-death comparator gives survival .8261/.9089/.9632 for
R4/8/16 at T266/347/432; the harmonic certificate stays above1/2 while the
raw-rank certificate is zero. The fully informed finite-chain control is stronger,
as expected. Exact F2/F4 enumeration also confirms all local quadratic-block
response identities. These finite tests do not establish arbitrary-rank genericity,
the full latent lemma or Unique Games hardness.

The159 iteration contributes a valid conditional dependency gate: persistent
width/size increments depending only on strictly higher layers remain polynomial
for a fixed-depth finite horizon. Same-layer polynomial feedback does not.
Its easy recurrence does not verify the source's hard sampling/lifting assumptions
or repair the cycle18 optimizer/purification certificate.

**Best next allocation:** independently reconstruct102's full latent alphabet
lemma and dimension-uniform Grassmann-to-shortcode import, including adaptive
linear lifts and quantitative construction cost. If these survive, seek a succinct
noise/decoder representation with useful acquired parameters. For159, audit the

## Main result candidate

For characteristic-p field pairs K^p⊆H⊆K and L^p⊆C⊆L with relative p-rank at least two, an Fp-linear isomorphism K*/H*→L*/C* preserving only pure-symbol vanishing in the relative degree-two Steinberg quotient is r·α_* for a unique field-pair isomorphism α and r∈Fp*. The complete derivation handles infinite p-bases, common-scalar propagation, projective lifting, and multiplicativity. Root independently identified the relative-base strengthening and is reviewing the derivation.

Technical note: [RELATIVE_RECONSTRUCTION.md](../../../notes/frontier_number1/cycle19/RELATIVE_RECONSTRUCTION.md).

The local lemma has an added iff classification and explicit witness: for p-independent X,Y and nonmonomial U∈C(X),V∈C(Y), the intersection X C(Y/X)∩U C(V/U) is nonzero iff a unique n gives U^n=a+bX^n,V^n=c+dY^n. The intersection is then exactly C·(cU^n−aV^n)^r, with nr≡1 modp. This also yields a finite coordinate certificate and an O(p³) C-arithmetic support-test algorithm, with the coordinate oracle charged as supplied.

Concrete consequence: recover arbitrary height-one purely inseparable field pairs, and recover function fields over finite or arbitrary perfect named bases when the base is the perfect core. This provides a structural interface for exact birational/inseparable reconstruction; an integral model or an efficient acquisition algorithm is not supplied.

# Cycle19 probability bridge report

**Strongest supported result:** a complete finite strongly-Rayleigh response-to-sampler reduction with explicit total-variation error, mean-query count, and finite-bit implementation contract. Together with exact weaker-access lower bounds, it separates structural generation from acquired inference. This is incremental research capital; no consequential breakthrough or cross-family efficient acquisition theorem was established.

For `mu` on `{0,1}^n`, set `p(h)=E_{mu_h}X`. The old SR covariance row bound gives `Dp=Cov<=I/2`. After shifting Gaussian observations by `t/2`, the posterior flow is autonomous:

\[
 dH_t=(p(H_t)-\tfrac12\mathbf1)dt+dW_t.
\]

With `eta=T/K<=1`, certified coordinate mean error `zeta`, and endpoint thresholding, the independent derivation gives

\[
 \operatorname{TV}(\widehat\mu,\mu)
 \le n e^{-T/8}+\sqrt{7nT\eta/96+nT\zeta^2/2}.
\]

Choosing `T=8 log(2n/epsilon)`, `K=ceil(max(T,7nT^2/(12epsilon^2)))`, and `zeta=epsilon/(2sqrt(nT))` gives TV at most `epsilon` using `O(n log^2(n/epsilon)/epsilon^2)` complete mean queries. Mean acquisition, representation, field-dependent precision, and arithmetic costs remain explicitly charged. A separate finite-bit coupling contract supplies a stronger required numerical mean precision and polynomial random-bit count; it is not assumed free.

An independent Wiener-LSI proof gives `R(mu*Gaussian_s)<=s^2(exp(1/s)-1)` for every `s>0`. The smoothing is universally log-concave iff `s>=1/2`, with sharpness at the one-of-two SR law. The conjectured use of new093 is therefore unnecessary for this class. The response/innovation primitives are older than the release; generic SR and diffusion sampling already have primary prior art.

The exact acquisition counterexamples are:

- A hidden point mass is strongly Rayleigh with zero response, yet a blackbox point-mass value oracle requires at least `(1-epsilon)2^n-1` queries for uniform TV accuracy if no support point or sample is supplied.
- With `p=1/(1+exp(B))`, base samples cannot distinguish `delta_0` from `Bernoulli(p)` cheaply, but field `B` tilts their targets to `delta_0` and `Bernoulli(1/2)`. Adaptive stopping obeys `E_0 N >=(1/2-2epsilon)(1+exp(B))` for uniform tilted-inference accuracy. This does not imply hardness for averaged errors on typical diffusion trajectories.

221/222 Gibbs targets do not inherit SR response from their variational laws; an allowed221 positive-bond disorder event already violates pairwise negative covariance. The QAOA281 main source expressly provides no depth/finite-size modulus or efficient angle selection. Its linked SKValue formalization scope explicitly does not assert QAOA circuit-energy convergence. Thus no sampler acquisition was smuggled through these interfaces.

**Proof/verification status:** full scientific source231 read and internally reconstructed; 093/221/222/281 exact theorem/interface excerpts only, with unread decisive proofs clearly recorded. Peer `pde_gate10` independently rebuilt the finite Euler constants, LSI consequence, DPP interface and access obstructions in `notes/pde_gate10/cycle19/FINITE_RESPONSE_SAMPLER_INDEPENDENT_GATE.md`. This is internal reconstruction, not external validation. The peer's universal local-graph FUSF obstruction P3–P5 was independently checked here and saved in an owned gate note.

The final integer/rational finite-precision contract also passed the peer's G6 reconstruction, including parameter acquisition, the polynomial upper bound for path amplification, dyadic quantile approximation and endpoint anti-concentration. It remains an evaluator/quantile-service reduction with encoded input and field-magnitude costs charged; no generic evaluator acquisition was certified.

## 4. Accuracy bits and the area-law boundary

**Precision ingredient available.** The source recursion has fewer than J=32768(n+1)^3 levels, each adding at most 18(3/4)^L clean-workspace operator error. For epsilon=2^-B, set

    L=4(B+ceil log2(36J)),
    eta<=2^-B/[2(S+1)].

The skeleton contributes at most epsilon/2; slot errors contribute at most epsilon/2. Its width Q=O((n+1)^4L^2) and slot count S=O((n+1)^4L^3). Retaining the manuscript's actual Solovay--Kitaev recurrence epsilon_next<=C epsilon^(3/2), length_next<=5length gives word length O(log^4(2/xi)), since log_(3/2)5<4. The complete oracle circuit, addresses and structure generation are polynomial in n,B. The separate phase compiler preserves scalar phase, and clean error epsilon gives full diamond error at most 2epsilon. This accuracy-bit consequence was independently derived by pde_gate9, then checked here.

Q4 therefore removes a precision-cost concern within the supplied unrestricted-oracle model. It supplies no target table, physical bath controls or exact-zero Hamiltonian completeness. Detailed cost composition: `notes/pde_gate9/cycle19/BOOLEAN_UNITARY_RECONSTRUCTION_AND_PRECISION.md`, U9-U10; parameter derivation also in our Boolean note.

**265 precise scope.** The PEPS companion assumes fixed local dimension, strength, a full excitation gap and unique ground state on an open square grid. It gives polynomial bond dimension and normalized global vector error at most 1/L. Its introduction supplies the ground state Omega to the initial party; the tensors may depend arbitrarily on Omega. Its complete proof was not audited here.

The area-law positive replacement, section 3 Proposition `prop:positive`, was reconstructed: filter local h_i and subtract <Omega,h_i Omega>I to obtain M_i, then k_i=|M_i|/c*. One has k_i positive contractions, k_i Omega=0, sum_i k_i>=(H-E0)/c*, with stretched-exponential quasi-local tails exp(-cr^alpha), alpha=p/(p+1)<1. This is useful positive conversion at a supplied gapped-ground-state interface. It does not remove the imported debt:

- The full excitation gap is distinct from a normalized ground-energy promise gap. Nonlocal or degenerate hard sparse-PCP inputs are outside the theorem's hypotheses.
- Ground expectations are charged input data; no efficient centering or PEPS tensor acquisition is provided.
- At inverse-polynomial error, radius scales as log(N)^(1/alpha). A square-lattice ball has O(log(N)^(2/alpha)) sites. Its dense Hilbert-space row support q^O(log(N)^(2/alpha)) is not polynomially bounded by this result.
- There are O(volume) positive anchors. Normalizing their total operator by a worst-case extensive norm can lose an extensive gap factor. Exact annihilation may also be lost under truncation.

The same existential vectors in 277 and target tables in 283 cannot be silently reused as physical state preparations. Continuum bath preparation retains the earlier oscillator-control and finite-truncation acquisition debt. The actual general common-positive-encoding target remains OPEN.

## Arbitrary-mask progress and obstruction

For a fixed mask W, a value-independent circuit for (Z_W,Y)↦ZYᵀ can be contracted with X and reverse differentiated to recover every (XY)_ij in W. Total arithmetic charge is O(T+ND+|W|), plus acquisition P and stored tape. Value-dependent zero-pruned traces do not automatically supply that circuit.

Graia's optimal deterministic FSMM theorem does not prove this source product fast: at the dense N×D output endpoint its two sketches may both be identities, so the oracle multiplication is the original sparse ZYᵀ. Ambient n cannot be freely replaced by √nnz(input). The tempting rescaling shortcut was retracted.

There exist legal masks W with |W|=Θ(N^(15/8)) for which every independently charged complete-block cover costs Ω(N²), even allowing arbitrary inner-coordinate subsets and ideal full-block matrix multiplication. The proof uses one Bernoulli mask with a simultaneous rectangle-density bound. It does not restrict cross-block algebraic sharing or general circuits.

Proof and exact scope: [SELECTIVE_OUTPUT_AUDIT.md](</Users/harjas/Documents/ChatGPT/Literature Digestion/notes/frontier_tensor_bridge/cycle19/SELECTIVE_OUTPUT_AUDIT.md>).



## Newly verified primary prior

Alman–Vassilevska Williams, arXiv:2610.06783v1, submitted 5 October 2026, claims a deterministic requested-output algorithm using O(N²/D^0.063) operations on O(log N)-bit integers for D≤N^(1/18), |W|≤N²/√D, with general regime D≤N^ε for every ε<0.1204. It derives explicit truly subquadratic 3SUM and truly subcubic APSP algorithms. This updates the imported literature state; its reductions are not independently certified here.

Full technical reading of its identity, error cancellation, tiling, pruned leaf counts, box data structure, and bit costs shows that the threshold ε*=log4/(5log10) comes from its ten-term output-local packing. The minimal tile side is D^(8.3048...+o(1)). The new α value cannot be substituted to shrink this exponent below4. A different output-local identity/packing is required for D=N^(1/4).

Primary source: https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06783v1. Saved technical HTML and parsed text are under sources/frontier_tensor_bridge/cycle19/prior/.

## Strongest results from this pass

1. **An exact local test for the positive prism formula (317).** For monotone
   f<=g on a poset, the one-term right-prism prescription is a chain map on
   every simplex precisely when, for each comparable x<y, f(x)=g(x) or
   g(x)=g(y). The universal proof gives both necessity in degree 1 and
   sufficiency by an all-degree cancellation. It relaxes the manuscript's
   global constant-complement and sieve assumptions for this formula. An
   antichain complement with a right-adjoint retraction admits a single
   stationary contraction extending across arbitrary attaching targets.
   The fork a<b,a<c gives an explicit example beyond the original local
   lemma. Dropping the criterion fails on three vertices. This claim is
   proved at the normalized-chain level; its strict Gray realization uses
   the published tensor comparison. Novelty is unclaimed.

2. **A quadratic family of conditional chromatic losses (314).** The explicit
   class-two group F_p^d x Lambda^2 F_p^d, for odd p, has d generators,
   exponent p, and order p^{d(d+1)/2}. Every cyclic section then has order p,
   giving ell(G,H)=log_p[G:H]. Conditional on release Theorem 1.1, the full
   fixed-point loss from the trivial subgroup is d(d+1)/2 at every target
   height, exceeding the elementary-quotient lower bound by binom(d,2).
   The group proof is independent and complete; the chromatic lower bound
   remains conditional. The d=2 odd-primary gap is already visible as an
   unresolved comparison in Kuhn-Lloyd's checked prior source. No novelty
   claim is made for the consequence.

3. **A reusable profinite recognition mechanism (314).** The independently
   reconstructed Theorem 5.1 proof converts all open-subgroup weighted
   congruences plus a finite-section rank bound and homomorphism-count
   integrality into a bounded series of Z_p factors. The key discriminator
   is a count c_h tending to zero by congruences but to a nonzero p-adic
   limit if the remaining kernel has finite abelianization. These are
   infinite hypotheses, so this is not a finite pro-p group decision tool.

Exact evidence: the cyclic-chain engine computed all subgroup pairs for
C4 x C2, D8, and the order-27 exponent-three group. It found 19 subgroups
in the last case and verified every relative logarithmic length. The prism
engine checked all 5,839 monotone comparable pairs on chains with at most
five vertices; 1,158 pass, exactly matching the proved criterion. A finite
check validates only its supplied finite inputs. No separating spectrum was
constructed and no local Lean compilation was performed.

W-hessian-bridge12 | hessian bridge12

## Outcome and adoption status

The strongest immediate research interface is the explicit graph bundle in family050. Its algebraic symmetric-power filtration and analytic invariant-measure obstruction can be reused to derive two stronger statements:

1. **Uniform twist ratio:** there is ε₀>0 such that f_m*G⊗O(r,r) admits no smooth Griffiths-semipositive Hermitian metric whenever m≥1,r≥0 and r/m<ε₀. Every r≥1 member is nevertheless ample; every even n with nr−1≥3m has a very ample n-th tautological power. The opposite bound is constructive: E_{m,3m} is globally generated and supports a smooth semipositive metric by dual evaluation, and E_{m,3m+1} supports a strict metric by tensoring P. Hence both minimal metric twist budgets grow **linearly in m**, while fixedr1 suffices algebraically. The evaluation space has dimension81m²+54m+2; its basis/metric matrix was not explicitly acquired and no acquisition-runtime bound is claimed.
2. **Explicit nef/metric separation:** G is algebraically nef and non-ample, but for each fixed positive Kähler form ω there is ε*>0 below which no smooth Hermitian metric has Griffiths curvature lower bound−ε*ωId.

These are complete author derivations in notes/hessian_bridge12/cycle19/UNIFORM_HERMITIAN_POSITIVITY_GAP.md, submitted to root for independent reconstruction. **At this report version they remain pending that independent gate**, rather than being silently adopted. Both gaps are ineffective. The core graph construction, filtration and no-PSH-quadratic cone are the source's results. The exact uniform-ratio and nef-G formulations are our deductions; novelty is UNKNOWN.

A separate, **independently reconstructed programme PASS** identifies a quantitative route from older primary work. If a supplied positive separable Levi-test tensor Q has formal adjoint S≤−ηId, and its tangent contraction has fibre operator bound W_Q≤CId, every smooth Hermitian metric has lower-curvature error ε≥η/C. Du–Xie's actual §2.3 constants give exclusion of ε<5π/26 at their split model and ε<5π/52 throughout their stated witness neighbourhood, with their unscaled curvature and tangent-H normalization. Equality is not excluded. ## Source claims and our mathematical deductions

The manuscript's Theorem 1.1 claims one integer polynomial for every recursively
enumerable natural-tuple predicate, with exactly one complete natural witness
tuple on members and none on nonmembers. Internal rational codes, slacks,
branch selectors and inactive variables all count. Proposition 4.2 supplies
an intermediate-growth function through a square-index height window;
Sections 5–7 install a Pell power graph and accepting counter computation.
These are full-read author claims. No decisive manuscript defect was found
in this scoped reading, but that is not a complete independent composition or
kernel proof.

Our first deduction is exact hidden-index addition and multiplication from
the same bounded-error height interface. For positive canonical point codes
Q=C(k), R=C(l), S=C(r), write

`A=max(W(Q+R),W(Q−R))=W_(k+l)`.

Then addition is `W(S)=A`. For multiplication use

`L=A W(S) W1`,
`D=W(Q) W(R) max(W(S+P),W(S−P))`.

The exact identity is `log(L/D)=2a(kl−r)+E`, with `|E|≤6c`.
Under `6c<logκ<2a−6c`, the two polynomial inequalities
`L≤κD` and `D≤κL` hold exactly when r=kl. Every auxiliary is uniquely
fixed after the output; zero and inactive branches are explicitly gated and
zeroed. The interface is an arithmetic graph on natural-coded points, not a
Diophantine model over Q or an acquired graph from an ordinary integer index
to its representative. Complete derivation and charged representation sizes
are in `notes/logic_gate14/cycle19/HEIGHT_WINDOW_ARITHMETIC.md`.

The six-height method needs bounded additive error. The monotone integer
sequence `W_j=2^(8j²+floor(j^(3/2)))` has quadratic leading growth and vanishing
relative error, yet rejects true products for every fixed multiplicative
window eventually. For k=l=10, r=100, its perturbation is already 13 in base-2
logarithms, exceeding the test window 7. This identifies why Poonen's older
relative denominator estimate alone cannot replace the full-coordinate height
in this mechanism; it does not refute his theorem or all denominator methods.

Our second universal deduction is a search barrier. For any fixed nonrecursive
RE set and any single-fold polynomial representing it, every total recursive
witness-coordinate or bit-length bound is exceeded on infinitely many members.
Otherwise finite-box evaluation decides membership; finitely many exceptions
can be hard-coded. Ordinary DPRM representations already have this barrier.
Uniqueness therefore enables auditable certificates, not generic bounded
certificate acquisition. Conversely, existential fixed numerical constants
do not imply that no recursive compiler exists: one valid finite tuple can
be hard-coded. The missing interface was supplied constants, not an abstract
noncomputability theorem for the compiler.

# Cycle19: exact observations for noncommutative model acquisition

Date:7October2026. Worker:`metric_prior`, root-verified GPT-6.1Sol/max handle; no nested launches. Release pinned at `adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a`. All116–128 allocation rows, including the123 catalogue hole, have literal read scope and remaining obligations in `FAMILY_ASSESSMENT.json`. A retrieved source or formalization label is not a proof gate.

The strongest completed own object is an exact, coefficient-independent hitting tuple for **every proper recognizable noncommutative series with a promised state bound** in characteristic zero. It follows from the release's finite-word integral embedding and older multiplicity mechanism after adding a word-length marker. The marker proof was independently reconstructed by `frontier_check_hitting`; that worker then derived and executed an output-only exact matrix-oracle learner. A later independently reconstructed root result sharpens this detector to m scalar-grid queries, each of dimension2nm, as described below. The underlying Hankel/residual synthesis is established prior, and older deterministic quasipolynomial output-only reconstruction already applies to proper cyclic series. Historical priority of the precise polynomial observation composition is UNKNOWN. These are internal mathematical results, not external validation or statistical learning theorems.



## Completed construction and exact interface

Input is only an alphabet size n, an upper bound m, and an oracle for the final matrix value of an unknown series having some realization

    R(x)=u(I_m−Σ_i B_i x_i)^−1v=Σ_w(uB_wv)x_w.

The hidden matrices/vectors, word coefficients, axes, internal gates and coefficient heights are not supplied. The oracle must return exact field elements on rational jointly nilpotent matrix tuples. This realization semantics makes every requested inverse defined. An arbitrary syntactic rational formula with canceled but undefined inverse gates is a different interface.

Set d=(n−1)binom(m,2)+m. The release's triangular rational operators satisfy

    T_i[r,q]=(-1)^(r−q−1)/(r i^(r−q)), 0≤q<r<d.

Let S_d be multiplication by λ on F[λ]/(λ^d), and set W_i=S_d⊗T_i of dimension **D=d²=O(n²m⁴)**. The length-marked formal solution G_λ=Σ_w λ^|w| B_w h_w is nonzero in a scalar component whenever R is nonzero: a nonzero homogeneous slice cannot vanish under finite-word independence. The original m-state multiplicity bound still applies over F(λ), so a nonzero jet occurs below z^d. Its λ-degree is below d, and regular-representation descent preserves it. Therefore R(W) has a nonzero seed column. Products of d generators vanish, so proper-domain validity is automatic over every characteristic-zero field. Exact derivation and source locations are in `notes/metric_prior/cycle19/LENGTH_MARKER_RECOGNIZABLE_HITTING.md`.

The word-length marker is necessary for this first-column argument. The explicit three-state cyclic series

    (1−x2)(1−x1)^−1x1−2x2

has nonzero degree-one coefficients1,−2 but its unmarked integral-system scalar component is identically zero, with exact ODE solution (1,z,0). This refutes the naive infinite-series extension of finite-word faithfulness; it does not refute the release's acyclic theorem or full-matrix detection. Our code independently constructs the operators and verifies the failure and the marked repair at D36. Universal correctness comes from the proof, not this fixture.

Construction/output is polynomial, but response heights and oracle execution are charged. Dense query descriptions may require n(D+r)² records. Our learner postprocessing is polynomial in n,m and the total encoded exact response length; it is not polynomial in n,m alone for unbounded unknown rational coefficients. Neither positivity/nonnegative realization nor noisy/passive-sample learning follows. A safe seed-reachable restriction has dimension d(d+1)/2; it only improves a constant and was not used in the saved execution.

## 3. Exact conditional pin first moment, with two obstructions

For a random permutation Π satisfying every exterior-minor identity E∧^kΠ=∧^kK, K=EΠ, partition labels J⊔Q and condition every cycle to hit J. Exact block determinants give

    Pr(G)=det(I−K_QQ),
    E[Σ|G]=K_JJ+K_JQ(I−K_QQ)^{-1}K_QJ.

The stronger arbitrary-W determinant identity proves every entry of the next-pin mean. Independent Bernoulli transpositions satisfy the exterior premise by rank-one affinity and independence. Given rational K, one determinant and linear solve compute the conditional mean in O(n³) rational operations and polynomial bit complexity; rejection of a rare event is unnecessary.

Two valid counterexamples delimit this service. Uniform S4 and (1+χ_(2,2)/2)/24 have identical exterior moments but different identity probabilities1/24 and1/12: exterior information does not identify the law. One shared physical X flip on two spins is (12)(34); at probability1/3 it has E detΠ=1 but detEΠ=1/9, violating the premise. This refutes the literal computational-configuration transfer, not passive quantum memory generally.

Complete proof: `EXTERIOR_PIN_TRANSFER_AND_OBSTRUCTIONS.md`. Runnable exact standard-library evidence: `work/rounding_spectral/cycle19/exterior_pin_exact.py`, result `exterior_pin_exact.json`. It checks all70 minors, exact all-hit probability31/64 and the conditional2×2 mean. This finite evidence does not test either physical thermodynamic theorem. Alon–Kozma's older one-pin long-cycle formula is an inspected primary comparator, not a new result here.

Its loop/exposure, sparse geometry, deterministic flow pairing, weighted heat-energy cancellation and positive-field KMS passage were reconstructed in the full read. The hard-sphere source claims positive constant-orbital occupation in exact canonical Gibbs states at each sufficiently dilute fixed density and a positive temperature T_phys=ρ^{3/2}a^{5/2}/(4α₀^{5/2}), held fixed before volume tends to infinity. Its purification/trajectory, spatial moment, bridge, group, route and scaling composition was reconstructed.

No counterexample to either stated physical main claim was found in that full read. These major claims still merit a second hostile reconstruction of their geometric routing/heat-flow and trajectory-trace compositions. Ancilla implementation costs up to four occupied Gaussian ancillas and four measurements per attempt;physical preparation/energy/hardware are charged,Gaussian-free only means no non-Gaussian resource.
For collective Gaussian/free-preserving processing, the converse follows from rho_eta^tensor n <=(1+R_eta)^n sigma_+^tensor n gives p R_q<=(1+R_eta)^n-1, hence n>=log(1+pR_q)/log(1+R_eta)=Omega(eta^-4) for fixed target q>0 and constant p;repeat independent filters attains Theta(eta^-4). Approx output epsilon uses max(R_q-2epsilon,0) witness. Example eta=.1 to q=.5 gives p=1/2025,2025 mean independent attempts. This is fixed mixed-resource concentration,not pure-state distillation,QEC/fault-tolerant achievability,or experiment. N104-signed-gaussian-estimation | Signed Gaussian estimator for bounded outputs; no positive-sampler transfer
status=source_derived_unreviewed;COMPLETE supplied Y evidence package;branch-specific internal proof/audit status below,external correctness/priority unresolved;checks SOURCE-REPORTED unless marked otherwise.
Notation local.

For standard magic blocks with exact decompositions rho_i=(1+R_i)sigma_i^+-R_i sigma_i^-, convex-Gaussian terms,independent signs and Gaussian output sampler,absolute mass Gamma=prod_i(1+2R_i). For any efficiently computable |f(X)|<=1, signed estimator Y=Gamma*s*f(X) is unbiased and |Y|<=Gamma;Hoeffding sample cost O(Gamma² epsilon^-2 log(2/alpha)) for additive epsilon,failure alpha. With eta_i bounded away from1 and sum eta_i^4=O(log M), cost polynomial,including inverse-polynomial additive accuracy. This logarithmic regime is stronger for ESTIMATION than positive mixture's full-law condition n eta^4=O(epsilon).
Do not infer a full positive sampler: signed weights aren't probabilities,rare conditional probabilities can require exponential relative accuracy. Actual code/Monte Carlo is floating-point; source report logs rounding exclusion and a repaired diagnostic branch clipping issue,so stated exact-arithmetic estimator proof is separate from uncertified software execution.

N105-conditional-fermion-code-bound | Conditional loss-dependent fermionic code-preparation lower bound
status=source_derived_unreviewed;COMPLETE supplied Y evidence package;branch-specific internal proof/audit status below,external correctness/priority unresolved;checks SOURCE-REPORTED unless marked otherwise.
Notation local.

This is the one main result with an external mathematical premise: Tang et al., arXiv:2609.15059v1, Theorem 2 at zero non-Gaussian gates (only stated specialization imported;full proof not independently certified). For m-mode code with k>0 logical qubits, Majorana distance d_F>=3, F_G(C)<=[1-H2^-1(k/m)]^floor((d_F-1)/2),H2^-1 on [0,1/2], bounding overlap with any Gaussian mixture. If only non-Gaussian inputs are lossy standard blocks rho_i and Gaussian processing succeeds p with fidelity>=1-epsilon to specified pure codeword, positivity and tensor robustness domination imply p(1-epsilon)<=[prod_i(1+R_i)]F_G(C). Thus sum_i log(1+R_i)>=floor((d_F-1)/2)*[-log(1-H2^-1(k/m))]+log[p(1-epsilon)];at constant rate/success/fidelity,uniform small eta, n=Omega(d_F/eta^4).
Scope is that input-loss/Gaussian-processing architecture. No code-preparation achievability, arbitrary extra non-Gaussian gates/noisy hardware, or loss record. Theorems A-D do not depend on Tang or Chen-Liu; source says no new OpenAI math-release manuscript is a premise.
